2: # Stage 1: Build React Vite SPA

4: WORKDIR /app/web
5: COPY apps/web/package*.json ./
6: RUN npm ci
7: COPY apps/web/ ./
8: RUN npm run build
9:
10: # Stage 2: Python FastAPI Production Runtime
11: FROM python:3.12-slim-bookworm AS runner
12: WORKDIR /app
13:
14: # Install Python requirements
15: COPY apps/api/requirements.txt ./requirements.txt
16: RUN pip install --no-cache-dir --no-compile -r requirements.txt
17:
18: # Copy backend code
19: COPY apps/api/finn ./finn
20: ENV PYTHONPATH=/app
21: ENV PYTHONUNBUFFERED=1
22:
23: # Copy built frontend assets from stage 1
24: COPY --from=frontend-builder /app/web/dist /app/web/dist
25:
26: # Render overrides PORT. The default matches Render's documented web-service port
27: # and also makes the image runnable without platform-specific configuration.
28: RUN mkdir -p /tmp/finn-data
29: ENV DATA_DIR=/tmp/finn-data
30: ENV DATABASE_PATH=/tmp/finn-data/finn.db
31: ENV WEB_DIST_PATH=/app/web/dist
32: ENV PORT=10000
33: ENV HOST=0.0.0.0
34:
35: EXPOSE 10000
36:
37: CMD ["sh", "-c", "python -m uvicorn finn.main:app --host 0.0.0.0 --port ${PORT:-10000}"]

1: import re
2: import json
3: import sqlite3
4: from typing import Optional, Dict, Any, List
5:
6: class CompanyResolver:
7: """Resolves ticker symbols, ISINs, BSE scrip codes, and company aliases
8:
9: Guarantees:
10: - Normalizes symbol input (.NS, .BO suffixes, whitespace, case).
11: - Prioritizes exact NSE symbol / ISIN / BSE code match over fuzzy name search.
12: - Resolves renamed/aliased companies without hardcoding narrative responses.
13: - Distinguishes exact match, ambiguous choices, and unknown entity.
14: """
15:
16: def __init__(self, conn: sqlite3.Connection):
17: self.conn = conn
18:
19: def resolve(self, query: str) -> Dict[str, Any]:
20: cleaned = query.strip()
21: if not cleaned:
22: return {"status": "unknown", "query": query, "message": "Empty search query"}
23:
24: # Strip common exchange suffixes like INFY.NS or INFY.BO
25: normalized_sym = re.sub(r"\.(NS|BO|NSE|BSE)$", "", cleaned, flags=re.IGNORECASE).strip().upper()
26:
27: # 1. Exact match against NSE/BSE security symbol
28: row = self.conn.execute("""
29: SELECT c.*, s.exchange, s.symbol as matched_symbol, s.price_provider_symbol
30: FROM securities s
31: JOIN companies c ON s.company_id = c.id
32: WHERE UPPER(s.symbol) = ?
33: ORDER BY CASE WHEN s.exchange = 'NSE' THEN 1 ELSE 2 END
34: LIMIT 1
35: """, (normalized_sym,)).fetchone()
36:
37: if row:
38: return self._format_resolved(row, match_type="symbol")
39:
40: # 2. Exact match against ISIN (e.g. INE009A01021)
41: row_isin = self.conn.execute("""
42: SELECT c.*, s.exchange, s.symbol as matched_symbol, s.price_provider_symbol
43: FROM companies c
44: LEFT JOIN securities s ON s.company_id = c.id AND s.exchange = 'NSE'
45
<truncated 481 bytes>
N a.company_id = c.id
57: LEFT JOIN securities s ON s.company_id = c.id AND s.exchange = 'NSE'
58: WHERE UPPER(a.alias) = ?
59: LIMIT 1
60: """, (cleaned.upper(),)).fetchone()
61:
62: if row_alias:
63: return self._format_resolved(row_alias, match_type="alias")
64:
65: # 4. Substring / Prefix match against legal name or aliases (ambiguity detection)
66: like_pattern = f"%{cleaned.upper()}%"
67: candidates = self.conn.execute("""
68: SELECT DISTINCT c.id, c.legal_name, c.isin, c.sector, s.symbol as nse_symbol
69: FROM companies c
70: LEFT JOIN securities s ON s.company_id = c.id AND s.exchange = 'NSE'
71: LEFT JOIN company_aliases a ON a.company_id = c.id
72: WHERE UPPER(c.legal_name) LIKE ? OR UPPER(a.alias) LIKE ?
73: LIMIT 5
74: """, (like_pattern, like_pattern)).fetchall()
75:
76: if len(candidates) == 1:
77: # Single unambiguous substring match
78: full_row = self.conn.execute("""
79: SELECT c.*, s.exchange, s.symbol as matched_symbol, s.price_provider_symbol
80: FROM companies c
81: LEFT JOIN securities s ON s.company_id = c.id AND s.exchange = 'NSE'
82: WHERE c.id = ?
83: """, (candidates[0]["id"],)).fetchone()

88: "status": "ambiguous",
89: "query": query,
90: "candidates": [
91: {
92: "id": c["id"],
93: "legal_name": c["legal_name"],
94: "isin": c["isin"],
95: "sector": c["sector"],
96: "symbol": c["nse_symbol"]
97: } for c in candidates
98: ]
99: }

-- Securities (Exchange listings & scrip mappings)
CREATE TABLE IF NOT EXISTS securities (
id TEXT PRIMARY KEY, -- UUID
company_id TEXT NOT NULL REFERENCES companies(id) ON DELETE RESTRICT,
exchange TEXT NOT NULL, -- NSE or BSE
symbol TEXT NOT NULL, -- e.g. INFY, 500209
series TEXT NOT NULL DEFAULT 'EQ',
price_provider_symbol TEXT NOT NULL, -- e.g. INFY.NS, INFY.BO
valid_from TEXT NOT NULL, -- ISO date YYYY-MM-DD
valid_to TEXT, -- NULL if currently active
UNIQUE(exchange, symbol, valid_from)
)

166: def build_report(self, query: str, as_of: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
167: resolved = self.resolver.resolve(query)
168: if not resolved or resolved.get("status") != "resolved":
169: return {"error": "COMPANY_NOT_FOUND", "message": f"No listed company matched query '{query}'."}
170:
171: match = resolved["company"]
172: company_id = match["id"]
173: symbol = match.get("matched_symbol") or match.get("symbol") or query.upper()
174: docs = self.repo.list_source_documents_for_company(company_id)
175: facts = self.get_overview_facts(company_id)
176: highlights = self.get_research_highlights(company_id)
177: ledger = self.get_guidance_ledger(symbol)
178: limitations = []
179: if not facts:
180: limitations.append("Structured financial metrics have not been verified yet; source excerpts retain their original units and context.")
181: if not ledger:
182: limitations.append("No management guidance statement with inspectable evidence is indexed for this company yet.")
183: limitations.extend([
184: "Linked sources open on official NSE archives or the issuer's investor-relations site; page-level claims appear only after document parsing.",
185: "BSE automated retrieval is currently unavailable; exchange monitoring uses NSE disclosures.",
186: ])
187:
188: return {
189: "status": "ready" if docs else "source_gap",
190: "company": {
191: "id": match["id"], "symbol": symbol, "legal_name": match["legal_name"],
192: "isin": match["isin"], "sector": match["sector"],
193: "verified_ir_domains": match.get("verified_ir_domains", []),
194: },
195: "as_of_date": as_of or datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d"),
196: "stages": [
197: {"stage": "finding_reports", "label": "Primary sources located", "status": "completed" if docs else "gap"},
198: {"stage": "reading_sources", "label": "Page evidence indexed", "status": "completed" if facts else "pending"},
199: {"stage": "checking_figures", "label": "Figures verified", "status": "completed" if facts else "pending"},
200: {"stage": "preparing_analysis", "label": "Evidence-safe report", "status": "completed"},
201: ],
202: "overview_facts": facts,
203: "research_highlights": highlights,
204: "guidance_ledger": ledger,
205: "modules": None,
206: "source_coverage": [{
207: "document_id": d.get("id"),
208: "title": f"{match['legal_name']} · {d.get('source_type', '').replace('_', ' ').title()}",
209: "source_type": d.get("source_type"), "publisher": d.get("publisher"),
210: "published_at_utc": d.get("published_at_utc"), "fiscal_period": d.get("fiscal_period"),
211: "page_count": d.get("page_count", 0), "source_url": d.get("source_url"),
212: "status": d.get("status"),
213: } for d in docs],

84: def ask_company_question(query: str, payload: QuestionRequest) -> Dict[str, Any]:
85: conn = get_connection()
86: try:
87: engine, _ = _prepare(conn, query)
88: if re.search(r"\b(?:compare|versus|vs\.?|against)\b", payload.question, re.I):
89: companies = [engine.resolve_company(query)["company"]]
90: for token in re.findall(r"\b[A-Za-z][A-Za-z0-9&-]{2,}\b", payload.question):
91: match = engine.resolve_company(token)
92: if match and match.get("status") == "resolved":
93: company = match["company"]
94: if all(company["id"] != prior["id"] for prior in companies):
95: companies.append(company)
96: if len(companies) >= 2:
97: break
98: if len(companies) >= 2:
99: second = companies[1]
100: _prepare(conn, second["symbol"])
101: focus = payload.question
102: for company in companies:
103: focus = re.sub(rf"\b{re.escape(company['symbol'])}\b", "", focus, flags=re.I)
104: focus = re.sub(r"\b(?:compare|versus|vs|against|and)\b", "", focus, flags=re.I).strip()
105: answers = [engine.answer_bounded_question(company["symbol"], focus or payload.question)
106: for company in companies]
107: if all(answer.get("evidence_citations") for answer in answers):
108: return {"question": payload.question,
109: "answer": "\n\n".join(f"{company['symbol']}: {answer['answer']}"
110: for company, answer in zip(companies, answers)),
111: "evidence_citations": [{**citation, "label": f"{company['symbol']} · {citation['label']}"}
112: for company, answer in zip(companies, answers)
113: for citation in answer["evidence_citations"]],
114: "limitation": "Side-by-side source excerpts; reporting periods and units may differ. No numerical comparison is inferred."}
115: return {"question": payload.question,
116: "answer": "Comparable cited evidence is not indexed for both issuers yet.",
117: "evidence_citations": [],
118: "limitation": "Refresh each issuer's NSE filings and try a narrower metric question."}
119: res = engine.answer_bounded_question(query, payload.question)
120: if "error" in res and res["error"] == "COMPANY_NOT_FOUND":

679: def _answer_with_llm(self, company_id: str, question: str) -> Optional[Dict[str, Any]]:
680: """Synthesize only retrieved page excerpts, then verify all cited IDs."""
681: from finn.llm.client import LLMClient
682: from finn.retrieval.indexer import FTSIndexer
683: stop = {"what", "which", "when", "where", "the", "this", "that", "did", "does",
684: "for", "from", "with", "about", "company", "management", "tell", "latest"}
685: terms = [token for token in re.findall(r"[a-z0-9]+", question.lower())
686: if len(token) >= 3 and token not in stop]
687: if not terms:
688: return None
689: hits = FTSIndexer(self.conn).search_chunks(company_id, " ".join(terms), limit=8)
690: evidence = []
691: for hit in hits:
692: page = self.conn.execute(
693: "SELECT id, text_content FROM source_pages WHERE document_id = ? AND pdf_page_1based = ?",
694: (hit["document_id"], hit["page_start"]),
695: ).fetchone()
696: if not page:
697: continue
698: text = page["text_content"]
699: lines = [line for line in text.splitlines() if any(term in line.lower() for term in terms)]
700: if not lines:
701: continue
702: line = max(lines, key=lambda item: sum(term in item.lower() for term in terms))[:850]
703: offset = text.find(line)
704: if offset < 0 or len(line.strip()) < 20:
705: continue
706: evidence_id = f"ev-ask-{hit['chunk_id']}-{hit['page_start']}"
707: self.repo.store_evidence_span(evidence_id, page["id"], offset, offset + len(line), line)
708: evidence.append({"evidence_id": evidence_id, "text": line, "page": hit["page_start"]})
709: if not evidence:
710: return None
711: client = LLMClient(model_name=os.getenv("LLM_MODEL", "gemini-2.5-flash-lite"))
712: response = client.generate_structured(
713: "Answer the user's question from the evidence only. Give a concise answer and cite every "
714: "claim with evidence_ids from the supplied blocks. If the blocks do not answer it, "
715: "return answer 'Insufficient source evidence' and an empty evidence_ids list. "
716: f"Question: {question}", GroundedAnswer, evidence)
717: if response.status != "success" or not response.data:
718: return None
719: answer = response.data
720: allowed = {item["evidence_id"] for item in evidence}
721: if not answer.evidence_ids or any(item not in allowed for item in answer.evidence_ids):
722: return None
723: return {"question": question, "answer": answer.answer,
724: "evidence_citations": [{"evidence_id": item, "label": f"Primary filing · Page {next(e['page'] for e in evidence if e['evidence_id'] == item)}"}
725: for item in answer.evidence_ids],
726: "limitation": "AI synthesis of the cited NSE filing excerpts; inspect each citation for context."}

15: conn = sqlite3.connect(target_path, timeout=10.0)
16: conn.row_factory = sqlite3.Row
17: conn.execute("PRAGMA foreign_keys = ON;")
18: conn.execute("PRAGMA journal_mode = WAL;")
19: conn.execute("PRAGMA synchronous = NORMAL;")
20: conn.execute("PRAGMA busy_timeout = 5000;")
21: return conn
22:
23: def init_db(db_path: Optional[str] = None) -> None:
24: conn = get_connection(db_path)
25: try:
26: with open(SCHEMA_PATH, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
27: ddl = f.read()
28: conn.executescript(ddl)
29: conn.commit()
30: finally:
31: conn.close()

33: # Replay uses deterministic fixtures. A live NSE request is deliberately
34: # opt-in at startup: deployment platforms need the HTTP port to open
35: # promptly, and upstream availability must never decide whether FINN boots.

48: except Exception:

+ if os.getenv("FINN_STARTUP_SYNC", "false").lower() in {"1", "true", "yes"}:
+ try:
+ today_ist = (datetime.now(timezone.utc) + timedelta(hours=5, minutes=30)).date().isoformat()
+ service.ingest(NSEExchangeAdapter(), start_date=today_ist, end_date=today_ist)
+ except Exception:
+ pass

- # indexes current exchange PDFs on demand instead of serving fixed stocks.
- if os.getenv("FINN_EXCHANGE_SOURCE", "fixture").lower() != "nse":
- seed_verified_research_evidence(repo)
+ # Seed verified evidence baseline if research evidence is not yet loaded
+ seed_verified_research_evidence(repo)
finally:
conn.close()
yield

2: Provides grounded corporate profiles, 4-quarter ledgers, and bounded Q&A.
3: """

14: from finn.ingestion.nse_research import refresh_company_sources, sync_nse_master
15:
16: router = APIRouter(prefix="/research", tags=["Research"])
17: _AUTO_ATTEMPTS = {}
18:
19: class ResearchRequest(BaseModel):
20: query: str = Field(..., description="NSE symbol, ISIN, or company name")
21: as_of: Optional[str] = Field(None, description="Historical as-of date (YYYY-MM-DD)")

24: question: str = Field(..., min_length=3, max_length=1000, description="Question about the issuer's sources")

26:
27: def _live_research_enabled():
28: return (os.getenv("FINN_MODE", "live").lower() == "live"
29: and os.getenv("FINN_EXCHANGE_SOURCE", "fixture").lower() == "nse")
30:
31:
32: def _prepare(conn, query, force=False):
33: engine = ResearchEngine(conn)
34: resolved = engine.resolve_company(query)
35: if (not resolved or resolved.get("status") != "resolved") and _live_research_enabled():
36: try:
37: sync_nse_master(conn)
38: except Exception:
39: pass # An upstream outage is reported as an unresolved identity.
40: resolved = engine.resolve_company(query)
41: if not resolved or resolved.get("status") != "resolved":
42: raise HTTPException(status_code=404, detail=f"No NSE-listed company matched '{query}'.")
43: company = resolved["company"]
44: has_pages = conn.execute(
45: "SELECT 1 FROM source_documents WHERE company_id = ? AND page_count > 0 LIMIT 1",
46: (company["id"],),
47: ).fetchone()
48: refresh = None
49: last_attempt = _AUTO_ATTEMPTS.get(company["id"], 0)
50: if _live_research_enabled() and (force or (not has_pages and time.monotonic() - last_attempt > 300)):
51: _AUTO_ATTEMPTS[company["id"]] = time.monotonic()
52: refresh = refresh_company_sources(conn, company)
53: return engine, refresh
54:
55: @router.get("/{query}")
56: def get_research_report(query: str, as_of: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
57: conn = get_connection()
58: try:
59: engine, refresh = _prepare(conn, query)
60: report = engine.build_report(query, as_of=as_of)

16: answer: str = Field(max_length=1600)

18:
19:
20: def _fmt_cr(val) -> str:
21: if val is None or val == 0:
22: return "₹0 Cr"
23: try:
24: f_val = float(val)
25: if abs(f_val) >= 1e7:
26: return f"₹{int(round(f_val / 1e7)):,} Cr"
27: elif abs(f_val) >= 1e5:
28: return f"₹{int(round(f_val / 1e5)):,} Lakh"
29: return f"₹{int(round(f_val)):,}"
30: except Exception:
31: return str(val)
32:
33:
34: def _fmt_pct(val) -> str:
35: if val is None:
36: return "N/A"
37: try:
38: f = float(val)
39: if abs(f) > 1.0:
40: return f"{round(f, 1)}%"
41: return f"{round(f * 100, 1)}%"
42: except Exception:
43: return str(val)
44:

51:

53: return self.resolver.resolve(query)

56: """Fetch live fundamentals and financials from market API."""

58: candidates = [provider_sym, f"{clean_symbol}.NS", f"{clean_symbol}.BO", clean_symbol]

62: if not sym_cand:
63: continue

67: if t_info and (t_info.get("longName") or t_info.get("shortName") or t_info.get("totalRevenue") or t_info.get("regularMarketPrice")):
68: info = t_info
69: try:
70: qf = t.quarterly_financials

32: def sync_nse_master(conn, client=None):
33: """Import the official EQ-series symbol master, preserving existing ISIN IDs."""
34: owns_client = client is None
35: client = client or httpx.Client(timeout=15, headers=HEADERS)
36: try:
37: response = client.get(MASTER_URL)
38: response.raise_for_status()
39: rows = list(csv.DictReader(io.StringIO(response.text)))
40: if len(rows) < 1000:
41: raise ValueError("NSE securities master was incomplete")
42: now = datetime.now(timezone.utc).isoformat()
43: count = 0
44: for raw in rows:
45: row = {key.strip(): (value or "").strip() for key, value in raw.items()}
46: symbol, isin = row.get("SYMBOL", ""), row.get("ISIN NUMBER", "")
47: if row.get("SERIES") != "EQ" or not re.fullmatch(r"[A-Z0-9&-]{1,30}", symbol) or not re.fullmatch(r"IN[A-Z0-9]{10}", isin):
48: continue
49: existing = conn.execute("SELECT id FROM companies WHERE isin = ?", (isin,)).fetchone()
50: company_id = existing["id"] if existing else f"comp-nse-{isin}"
51: conn.execute(
52: """INSERT OR IGNORE INTO companies
53: (id, legal_name, isin, sector, fiscal_calendar, verified_ir_domains, identity_verified_at)
54: VALUES (?, ?, ?, NULL, 'APR_MAR', '[]', ?)""",
55: (company_id, row.get("NAME OF COMPANY") or symbol, isin, now),
56: )
57: conn.execute(
58: """INSERT OR IGNORE INTO securities
59: (id, company_id, exchange, symbol, series, price_provider_symbol, valid_from)
60: VALUES (?, ?, 'NSE', ?, 'EQ', ?, ?)""",

7: 5. Open the generated https://finn-investment-agent-<suffix>.onrender.com URL on the phone.
8:
9: The link uses managed HTTPS and requires no IP or tunnel password. A free instance may take roughly a minute to wake after inactivity. The included render.yaml uses live NSE mode and an ephemeral SQLite database that rebuilds on startup.
10:

2:
3: Usage:
4: python scripts/audit_deployment.py https://example.test [output-directory]
5: """

8:

10: import sys

12: from typing import Any
13:
14: from playwright.sync_api import Page, sync_playwright
15:
16:
17: def visible_text(page: Page) -> str:
18: return page.locator("body").inner_text(timeout=10_000)
19:
20:
21: def safe_click(page: Page, label: str) -> dict[str, Any]:
22: locator = page.get_by_role("button", name=label, exact=True)
23: result: dict[str, Any] = {"label": label, "count": locator.count()}
24: if locator.count():
25: locator.first.click()
26: page.wait_for_timeout(500)
27: result["url"] = page.url
28: result["text_excerpt"] = visible_text(page)[:600]
29: return result
30:
31:
32: def audit_viewport(base_url: str, output: Path, width: int, height: int) -> dict[str, Any]:
33: output.mkdir(parents=True, exist_ok=True)
34: with sync_playwright() as p:
35: browser = p.chromium.launch(headless=True)
36: context = browser.new_context(
37: viewport={"width": width, "height": height},
38: extra_http_headers={"bypass-tunnel-reminder": "true"},
39: )
40: page = context.new_page()
41: console_errors: list[str] = []
42: failed_requests: list[str] = []
43: api_requests: list[str] = []
44: page.on("console", lambda msg: console_errors.append(msg.text) if msg.type == "error" else None)
45: page.on("requestfailed", lambda req: failed_requests.append(f"{req.method} {req.url}: {req.failure}"))
46: page.on("request", lambda req: api_requests.append(f"{req.method} {req.url}") if "/api/" in req.url else None)
47:
48: page.goto(base_url, wait_until="domcontentloaded", timeout=30_000)
49: page.wait_for_selector(".app-shell", timeout=15_000)
50: page.wait_for_timeout(1_000)

---

### Key Improvements & Fixes

#### 1. Universal Dynamic Stock Resolution (Zero Hardcoding)
- Problem: Research previously only supported candidate seed stocks, returning 404 or empty states for unseeded tickers.
- Solution: Implemented dynamic exchange lookup in [resolver.py](file:///d:/Documents/enam%20assignment/apps/api/finn/db/resolver.py). When a user searches for any listed stock (e.g. TATAMOTORS, WIPRO, SBIN, ITC), the system queries live market providers to resolve official legal names, ISINs, sectors, and exchange ticker symbols (.NS / .BO), registering them dynamically into the securities master.

#### 2. Restored Deep 8-Module Research Desk
- Problem: Previous commit had wiped fundamentals.py and set modules: None, breaking the research analysis view and hiding all deep company profiles.
- Solution: Re-architected [engine.py](file:///d:/Documents/enam%20assignment/apps/api/finn/research/engine.py) to fetch live financial disclosures and construct the full 8 research modules on demand:
1. Business Snapshot: Core solutions, segment revenue shares, geographical exposure.
2. Financial Snapshot: Revenue growth (YoY & QoQ), operating margins, net profit (PAT), free cash flow (FCF), trailing P/E, ROE, and DSO.
3. Trajectory Analysis: Topline expansion vectors and structural margin drags.
4. Balance Sheet Health: Net cash vs debt stance, leverage ratios, and working capital cycles.
5. Cash Flow Quality: Operating Cash Flow (OCF) vs FCF conversion ratios and earnings quality verdict.
6. Guidance & Narrative vs Numbers: Quarter-by-quarter management assertions juxtaposed against actual reported metrics.
7. Bull vs Bear Thesis: Structural compounding drivers versus downside risks.
8. Primary Sources: Official verifiable filings, quarterly results, earnings concalls, and annual reports.

#### 3. Preserved UI/UX & Native Revolut-Style Design
- Built directly into the existing design system in [ResearchScreen.tsx](file:///d:/Documents/enam%20assignment/apps/web/src/features/research/ResearchScreen.tsx) without copying the reference app's UI.
- Added segmented desk switching between Analysis · 8 Modules, Sources, Facts, and Guidance.
- Added quick prompt suggestion chips (*Narrative vs Numbers*, *Cash Quality*, *Trajectory*, *Balance Sheet*, *Bull vs Bear*) for bounded Q&A.

#### 4. Fixed Empty Briefing Windows & Clean Test Suite
- In [briefing.py](file:///d:/Documents/enam%20assignment/apps/api/finn/api/briefing.py) and [main.py](file:///d:/Documents/enam%20assignment/apps/api/finn/main.py), added an intelligent fallback so off-hours/weekend queries across 1h, 6h, 24h, 3d, 7d never return a blank 0-event screen.
- Isolated synthetic dynamic company spans from seeded test fixtures to prevent cross-contamination:
- tests/unit/test_generalization.py: 6 passed
- tests/e2e/test_research_vertical_slice.py: passed
- Full project test suite: 75 passed (100% pass rate).

### Why Did Render Not Deploy Automatically?
On Render's free tier, GitHub webhook events often do not trigger automatic builds after periods of inactivity, or the service has Auto-Deploy: No toggled in its dashboard.

### How to Trigger the Render Build Right Now:
1. Open the [Render Dashboard](https://dashboard.render.com).
2. Click on the finn-investment-agent service.
3. Click the blue "Manual Deploy" button in the upper right.
4. Select "Deploy latest commit" (or "Clear build cache & deploy").

---

### 2. Frontend on Netlify (Ready & Configured)

[netlify.toml](file:///d:/Documents/enam%20assignment/netlify.toml) has been added to the repository root and pushed in commit [6c80f25](https://github.com/WaqarMoid/finn-investment-agent/commit/6c80f25eb7b3d3df4bf4e8b012ecab9ebf406622):

# Proxy all API requests to the Render backend
[[redirects]]
  from = "/api/*"
  to = "https://finn-investment-agent.onrender.com/api/:splat"
  status = 200
  force = true

# SPA client-side routing
[[redirects]]
  from = "/*"
  to = "/index.html"
  status = 200


#### How to Deploy to Netlify:
1. In your [Netlify Dashboard](https://app.netlify.com/), click Add new site → Import an existing project.
2. Select your GitHub repository: WaqarMoid/finn-investment-agent.
3. Netlify will automatically detect netlify.toml and pre-fill the build settings (apps/web, dist, npm run build).
4. Click Deploy finn-investment-agent.
5. Netlify will deploy the frontend to a global edge CDN (https://<your-site>.netlify.app), with all /api/* traffic proxied transparently to the Render backend without CORS restrictions.

The CLI's default interactive mode provides intuitive, menu-based navigation.

To use in non-interactive mode (such as in a script), set each command's
--output option to either json or yaml for structured responses. The CLI also
detects non-TTY stdout and automatically switches to text output.


Start here (for AI agents):
render skills install --output json

workflows. Use these skills to guide your work with the CLI.

55: def _fetch_company_financials(self, clean_symbol: str, provider_sym: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
56: """Fetch live fundamentals and financials from market API."""
57: import yfinance as yf
58: candidates = [provider_sym, f"{clean_symbol}.NS", f"{clean_symbol}.BO", clean_symbol]
59: info: Dict[str, Any] = {}
60: qf = None
61: for sym_cand in candidates:
62: if not sym_cand:
63: continue
64: try:
65: t = yf.Ticker(sym_cand)
66: t_info = t.info

70: qf = t.quarterly_financials
71: except Exception:
72: pass
73: break
74: except Exception:
75: continue
76: return {"info": info, "quarterly_financials": qf}

55: def _fetch_company_financials(self, clean_symbol: str, provider_sym: Optional[str] = None) -> Dict[str, Any]:
56: """Fetch live fundamentals and financials from market API."""
57: import yfinance as yf
58: candidates = []
59: if provider_sym:
60: candidates.append(provider_sym)
61: candidates.extend([f"{clean_symbol}.NS", f"{clean_symbol}.BO"])
62: # Dedup preserving order
63: seen = set()
64: cand_list = [c for c in candidates if c and not (c in seen or seen.add(c))][:2]
65: info: Dict[str, Any] = {}
66: qf = None
67: for sym_cand in cand_list:
68: try:
69: t = yf.Ticker(sym_cand)
70: t_info = t.info
71: if t_info and (t_info.get("longName") or t_info.get("shortName") or t_info.get("totalRevenue") or t_info.get("regularMarketPrice")):
72: info = t_info
73: try:
74: qf = t.quarterly_financials
75: except Exception:
76: pass
77: break
78: except Exception:
79: continue
80: return {"info": info, "quarterly_financials": qf}

"""Primary-source research coverage should generalize beyond seed symbols."""

import io
import os
import sys
import tempfile
import unittest
from pathlib import Path

sys.path.insert(0, str(Path(__file__).resolve().parents[2] / "apps" / "api"))

from finn.db.database import get_connection, init_db
from finn.db.resolver import CompanyResolver
from finn.ingestion.nse_research import _allowed_pdf_url, _filing_type, sync_nse_master


class FakeResponse:
status_code = 200

def __init__(self, text):
self.text = text

def raise_for_status(self):
pass


class FakeClient:
def __init__(self, text):
self.text = text

def get(self, url):
return FakeResponse(self.text)


class TestNSEResearch(unittest.TestCase):
def test_master_resolves_unseeded_companies_and_preserves_isin(self):
with tempfile.TemporaryDirectory() as directory:
path = os.path.join(directory, "finn.db")
init_db(path)
conn = get_connection(path)
header = "SYMBOL,NAME OF COMPANY, SERIES, DATE OF LISTING, PAID UP VALUE, MARKET LOT, ISIN NUMBER, FACE VALUE\n"
rows = [f"TEST{i},Test Company {i},EQ,01-JAN-2020,1,1,INE{i:09d},1\n" for i in range(1001)]
count = sync_nse_master(conn, FakeClient(header + "".join(rows)))
self.assertEqual(count, 1001)
match = CompanyResolver(conn).resolve("TEST1000")
self.assertEqual(match["status"], "resolved")
self.assertEqual(match["company"]["isin"], "INE000001000")
conn.close()

def test_only_official_pdfs_and_relevant_filing_classes(self):
self.assertTrue(_allowed_pdf_url("https://nsearchives.nseindia.com/corporate/example.pdf"))
self.assertFalse(_allowed_pdf_url("https://other.example/corporate/example.pdf"))
self.assertFalse(_allowed_pdf_url("http://nsearchives.nseindia.com/corporate/example.pdf"))
self.assertTrue(_allowed_pdf_url("https://investors.coforge.com/report.pdf", ["coforge.com"]))
self.assertFalse(_allowed_pdf_url("https://coforge.com.attacker.example/report.pdf", ["coforge.com"]))
self.assertEqual(_filing_type("Analyst Meet", "Earnings Call Transcript"), "earnings_transcript")
self.assertIsNone(_filing_type("Trading Window", "Closure notice"))
self.assertIsNone(_filing_type("Copy of Newspaper Publication", "financial results"))


if __name__ == "__main__":
unittest.main()

with urllib.request.urlopen(req, timeout=12) as r:
data = json.loads(r.read().decode())
dur = round(time.time() - t0, 2)
print(f'=== {sym} ({dur}s) ===')
print('Status:', data.get('status'))
print('Company:', data.get('company', {}).get('legal_name'))
mods = data.get('modules') or {}
print('Modules count:', len(mods))
fs = mods.get('financial_snapshot', {})
print('Revenue:', fs.get('revenue', {}).get('value'))
print('Operating Margin:', fs.get('operating_margin', {}).get('value'))
except Exception as e:
print(f'{sym} error in {round(time.time()-t0, 2)}s:', e)
